The Release, Captured Once with Claude on Real GitHub

ARTIE walks release.goal.md once on real GitHub, the model choosing each move from what the gateway offers; artie generate keeps the walk as release.heart.yaml.

---
run:
  inputs:
    scm:
      value: http://localhost:8093/
      given: given
      default: http://localhost:8098/
    pull:
      value: '2328'
      given: given
      question: Which pull request?
    owner:
      value: JogoShugh
      given: given
      default: acme
    repo:
      value: heart-github-test
      given: given
      default: web
    watcher:
      value: http://localhost:8099/
      given: default
      default: http://localhost:8099/
    jenkins:
      value: http://localhost:8170/
      given: default
      default: http://localhost:8170/
    dev:
      value: http://localhost:8190/
      given: default
      default: http://localhost:8190/
    environment:
      value: '1'
      given: default
      default: '1'
    book:
      value: The Hobbit
      given: default
      default: The Hobbit
    author:
      value: J. R. R. Tolkien
      given: default
      default: J. R. R. Tolkien
    renewable:
      value: Matilda
      given: default
      default: Matilda
    problem:
      value: missing-pages
      given: default
      default: missing-pages
    comment:
      value: Pages 12 to 14.
      given: default
      default: Pages 12 to 14.
  source: |-
    act(intent = "Get pull request $pull of $owner/$repo")
    enter("{watcher}") {
    act(intent = "watch http://localhost:8093/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/pulls/2328 and tell me when it offers scm:merge")
    onceAnswered("watch") {
    enter("{pr}") {
    act(intent = "merge the pull request by squashing")
    enter("{jenkins}") {
    act(intent = "build commit 30a0fa96e3eed2ae20b2b5fa7613a115d56312a3 and tell me how it goes")
    onceAnswered("build") {
    act(intent = "follow the in-reply-to link to the build")
    runScript("ci:smoke-test")
    given({smoke.passed}) {
    enter("{dev}") {
    act(intent = "get environment $environment")
    act(intent = "promote commit 30a0fa96e3eed2ae20b2b5fa7613a115d56312a3, citing 5 of 5 scenarios passed")
  marks:
    '1':
      as: pr
      intent: Open pull request ${pull} of ${owner}/${repo}
    '2':
      enter: ${watcher}
    '3':
      ask: watch
      as: watch
      intent: ask to be told when {pr} offers scm:merge
    '4':
      once-answered: watch
    '5':
      enter: ${pr.@self}
    '6':
      as: merged
      intent: merge it by squashing
    '7':
      enter: ${jenkins}
    '8':
      ask: build
      as: build
      intent: build commit {merged.merge_commit_sha}, asking to be told how it goes
    '9':
      once-answered: build
    '10':
      as: built
      intent: follow its in-reply-to link to the build
    '11':
      run-script: ci:smoke-test
      as: smoke
      on-link: ci:deployment
      inputs:
        book: ${book}
        author: ${author}
        renewable: ${renewable}
        problem: ${problem}
        comment: ${comment}
    '12':
      given:
        - at: smoke
          field: passed
          is: true
      otherwise:
        - at: ${pr.@self}
          steps:
            - act: scm:comment
              fields:
                body: 'Not promoted to Dev: smoke scenarios ${smoke.failed} failed.'
    '13':
      enter: ${dev}
    '14':
      fromIntent: open environment ${environment} and promote commit {merged.merge_commit_sha}, citing {smoke.passing} of {smoke.of} scenarios passed
    '15':
      as: promotion
      intent: open environment ${environment} and promote commit {merged.merge_commit_sha}, citing {smoke.passing} of {smoke.of} scenarios passed
  moves:
    - 'why: Open the pull request using the pulls-get form at the start.'
    - 'Get pull request 2328 of JogoShugh/heart-github-test: acted scm:pulls-get, done, at http://localhost:8093/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/pulls/2328'
    - 'why: Pull request 2328 is open and held, so the step is complete.'
    - 'done: Open pull request 2328 of JogoShugh/heart-github-test'
    - 'why: Held watcher offers watcher:watch; ask to be told when the pull request offers scm:merge.'
    - 'watch http://localhost:8093/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/pulls/2328 and tell me when it offers scm:merge: acted watcher:watch, new resource, at http://localhost:8099/watches/1'
    - 'why: The watch was created at watches/1 and is watching for scm:merge, so the step''s work is done.'
    - 'done: ask to be told when http://localhost:8093/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/pulls/2328 offers scm:merge'
    - 'why: The pull request now offers scm:merge, so the watch condition holds; merge it using squash.'
    - 'merge the pull request by squashing: acted scm:merge, done, at http://localhost:8093/repos/JogoShugh/heart-github-test/pulls/2328'
    - 'why: The squash merge was acted and the pull request now shows merged: true, so the step''s work is complete.'
    - 'done: merge it by squashing'
    - 'why: Jenkins offers ci:build; start the build of the merge commit and ask to be told the outcome.'
    - 'build commit 30a0fa96e3eed2ae20b2b5fa7613a115d56312a3 and tell me how it goes: acted ci:build, new resource, at http://localhost:8170/builds/1'
    - 'why: The build of the merge commit was created at builds/1 with a reply-to to be told the outcome, so the step''s work is done.'
    - 'done: build commit 30a0fa96e3eed2ae20b2b5fa7613a115d56312a3, asking to be told how it goes'
    - 'why: The build''s reply is answered (completed); follow its in-reply-to link to the build.'
    - 'follow the in-reply-to link to the build: followed hr:in-reply-to, done, at http://localhost:8170/builds/1'
    - 'why: The in-reply-to link was followed and the build at builds/1 is held, so the step is complete.'
    - 'done: follow its in-reply-to link to the build'
    - 'why: Smoke passed (5 of 5); open environment 1 first, then promote there.'
    - 'get environment 1: acted env:get-environment, done, at http://localhost:8190/environments/1'
    - 'why: Environment 1 is held and offers env:promote; smoke passed 5 of 5.'
    - 'promote commit 30a0fa96e3eed2ae20b2b5fa7613a115d56312a3, citing 5 of 5 scenarios passed: acted env:promote, done, at http://localhost:8190/environments/1'
    - 'why: The promote act ran and environment 1 now serves commit 30a0fa96..., so the step''s work is complete.'
    - 'done: open environment 1 and promote commit 30a0fa96e3eed2ae20b2b5fa7613a115d56312a3, citing 5 of 5 scenarios passed'
  model:
    name: claude-sonnet-5-5
    url: https://api.anthropic.com
  usage:
    calls: 20
    input: 33140
    output: 2980
    cache_read: 29424
    cache_write: 2911
